namespace = bci_events

# 0001 init
# 1000 cheat  2000 trait  3000 perk  4000 skill
# 5000 nickname  6000 faith index  7000 culture
# Faith index is 6000-6007, 8 choices a page. Sublists: 6170, 6180, 6230, 6280, 6290, 6310, 6591, 6600.
# 8000 secret  8100 relation  8200 sexual  9000 dna
# Child of 1000 is 1100, child of 1100 is 1110, child of 1110 is 1111.
# Further pages of the same list use the next free id in that slot.
# Total options include Next, Main menu, and End: 10 by default, 11 for nickname, trait, faith, and culture. Hub bci_events.0001 is 12.
# bci_events.0001 is the full main menu and is not split.
# Trait index 2000 is one page. Winter soldier is on the commander list.
# Court traits are on the other list.
# Perk is 3000 (+1 point) and 3001 (-1 point) per lifestyle. No perk-tree page.
# Secret is one page at 8000.
# Heal is send_options on bci_heal, not an event list.
# Culture is one list at 7000. Pages use the ones digit, then the next hundred.
# Nicknames are one list at 5000. Pages use the ones digit, then the next hundred.
# Christianity is one list: 6180, 6181, 6182, 6700. Buddhism is 6170-6171. Islam is 6290-6292.

bci_events.0001 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_events.0001
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_cheat
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_trait
		trigger_event = bci_events.2000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_perk
		trigger_event = bci_events.3000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_skill
		trigger_event = bci_events.4000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_nick
		trigger_event = bci_events.5000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_faith
		trigger_event = bci_events.6000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_culture
		trigger_event = bci_events.7000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_secret
		trigger_event = bci_events.8000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_relation
		trigger = {
			NOT = { scope:actor_scope = scope:currency_scope }
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.8100
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_sexual
		trigger_event = bci_events.8200
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_dna
		trigger = {
			scope:currency_scope = { bci_protected_dna_trigger = no }
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.9000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_events.end
	}
}

bci_events.1000 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_menu_cheat_t
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_change_influence
		scope:currency_scope = {
			change_influence = 1000
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_add_piety
		scope:currency_scope = {
			add_piety = 1000
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_add_prestige
		scope:currency_scope = {
			add_prestige = 1000
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_add_dynasty_prestige
		scope:currency_scope = {
			dynasty = { add_dynasty_prestige = 1000 }
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_add_gold
		scope:currency_scope = {
			add_gold = 1000
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_add_legitimacy
		scope:currency_scope = {
			add_legitimacy = 1000
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_sub_tyranny
		trigger = {
			scope:currency_scope = { tyranny > 0 }
		}
		scope:currency_scope = {
			add_tyranny = -1000
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1000
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_next
		trigger_event = bci_events.1001
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_back
		trigger_event = bci_events.0001
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_events.end
	}
}

bci_events.1002 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_menu_cheat_t
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = add_glory_debug_low_tt
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc_feature = accolades }
				if = {
					limit = { is_acclaimed = yes }
					accolade = { add_glory = monumental_glory_gain }
				}
				every_active_accolade = { add_glory = monumental_glory_gain }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1002
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_next
		trigger_event = bci_events.1003
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_back
		trigger_event = bci_events.0001
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_events.end
	}
}

bci_events.1003 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_menu_cheat_t
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_long_life
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = bci_long_life }
				remove_character_modifier = bci_long_life
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = bci_long_life
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.1003
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_short_life
		scope:currency_scope = {

bci_events.2100 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_cat_personality
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_ambitious
		trait = ambitious
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = ambitious }
				remove_trait = ambitious
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = content
				add_trait = ambitious
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_arbitrary
		trait = arbitrary
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = arbitrary }
				remove_trait = arbitrary
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = just
				add_trait = arbitrary
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_arrogant
		trait = arrogant

			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = arrogant }
				remove_trait = arrogant
			}
			else = {

			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {

			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_callous
		trait = callous
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = callous }
				remove_trait = callous
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = compassionate
				remove_trait = sadistic
				add_trait = callous
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_calm
		trait = calm
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = calm }
				remove_trait = calm
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = wrathful
				add_trait = calm
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_chaste
		trait = chaste
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = chaste }
				remove_trait = chaste
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = lustful
				add_trait = chaste
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_compassionate
		trait = compassionate
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = compassionate }
				remove_trait = compassionate
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = callous
				remove_trait = sadistic
				add_trait = compassionate
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2100
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_next
		trigger_event = bci_events.2101
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_back
		trigger_event = bci_events.0001
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_events.end

					NOT = { has_trait = education_diplomacy }
				}

						value = 25
					}

								value >= 75

						}

				else_if = {

						NOT = {

								trait = infirm track = infirm
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = infirm track = infirm
						value = 25
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = infirm
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2402
	}
	option = {

		scope:currency_scope = {

				add_trait = scarred
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = scarred track = scarred
							value >= 100
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = scarred track = scarred
								value >= 50
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = scarred track = scarred
						value = 50
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = scarred track = scarred
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = scarred track = scarred
						value = 50
					}

			}
			else = {
				add_trait = typhus
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2404
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_withering_mind
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = withering_mind }
				}
				add_trait = withering_mind
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
							value >= 100
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
								value >= 25
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
						value = 25
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
								value >= 50
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
						value = 25
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
								value >= 75
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
						value = 25
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = withering_mind track = withering_mind
						value = 25
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = withering_mind
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2404
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_wounded
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = wounded }
				}
				set_trait_rank = { trait = wounded rank = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_trait_rank = { trait = wounded rank < 3 } }
				change_trait_rank = { trait = wounded rank = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_trait_rank = { trait = wounded rank = 0 }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2404
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_next

	}
	option = {

						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = charioteer_red
						value = 50
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = charioteer_red
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2702
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_charioteer_white
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = charioteer_white }
				}
				add_trait = charioteer_white
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {

					NOT = { has_trait = child_of_concubine }
				}
				set_trait_rank = { trait = child_of_concubine rank = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_trait_rank = { trait = child_of_concubine rank < 2 } }
				change_trait_rank = { trait = child_of_concubine rank = 1 }

			else = {

							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = gallowsbait track = marauder
								value >= 60
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = gallowsbait track = marauder
						value = 20
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = gallowsbait track = marauder
								value >= 80
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = gallowsbait track = marauder
						value = 20
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = gallowsbait track = marauder
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = gallowsbait track = marauder
						value = 20
					}
				}
			}

			}

						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = violet_poet
							value >= 100
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = violet_poet
								value >= 33
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = violet_poet
						value = 33
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = violet_poet
								value >= 66
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = violet_poet
						value = 33
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = violet_poet
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = violet_poet
						value = 34
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				remove_trait = violet_poet
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2710
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_wild_oat
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = wild_oat }
				remove_trait = wild_oat
			}
			else = {
				add_trait = wild_oat
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2710
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_witch
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = witch }
				remove_trait = witch
			}
			else = {
				add_trait = witch
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2710
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_next
		trigger_event = bci_events.2700
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_menu_back
		trigger_event = bci_events.0001
	}
	option = {
		name = bci_events.end
	}

bci_events.2804 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_cat_other
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_tourney_participant
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = tourney_participant }
				}
				add_trait = tourney_participant
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = tourney_participant track = foot
							value >= 100
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = tourney_participant track = bow
							value >= 100
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = tourney_participant track = horse
							value >= 100
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = tourney_participant track = wit
							value >= 100
						}
					}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = foot
								value >= 30
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = foot
						value = 30
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = foot
								value >= 65
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = foot
						value = 35
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = foot
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = foot
						value = 35
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = bow
								value >= 30
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = bow
						value = 30
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = bow
								value >= 65
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = bow
						value = 35
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = bow
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = bow
						value = 35
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = horse
								value >= 30
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = horse
						value = 30
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = horse
								value >= 65
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = horse
						value = 35
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = horse
								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = horse
						value = 35
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = wit
								value >= 30
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = wit
						value = 30
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_trait_xp = {
								trait = tourney_participant track = wit
								value >= 65
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = wit
						value = 35
					}
				}
				else_if = {

								value >= 100
							}
						}
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = tourney_participant track = wit

					}
				}

			else = {

		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = administrative_court }
				}
				set_trait_rank = { trait = administrative_court rank = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_trait_rank = { trait = administrative_court rank < 2 } }
				change_trait_rank = { trait = administrative_court rank = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_trait_rank = { trait = administrative_court rank = 0 }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2804
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_diplomatic_court
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = diplomatic_court }
				}
				set_trait_rank = { trait = diplomatic_court rank = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_trait_rank = { trait = diplomatic_court rank < 2 } }
				change_trait_rank = { trait = diplomatic_court rank = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_trait_rank = { trait = diplomatic_court rank = 0 }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2804
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_intrigue_court
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = intrigue_court }
				}
				set_trait_rank = { trait = intrigue_court rank = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_trait_rank = { trait = intrigue_court rank < 2 } }
				change_trait_rank = { trait = intrigue_court rank = 1 }
			}
			else = {

			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.2804
	}
	option = {

			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = scholarly_court }
				}

bci_events.8000 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_menu_secret_t
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = secret_deviant
		trigger = {
			scope:currency_scope = { is_adult = yes }
		}
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { any_secret = { type = secret_deviant } }
				random_secret = {
					limit = { type = secret_deviant }
					remove_secret = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_secret = { type = secret_deviant }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.8000
	}
	option = {
		name = secret_homosexual

			scope:currency_scope = { is_adult = yes is_male = yes }

				random_secret = {
					limit = { type = secret_homosexual }
					remove_secret = yes

bci_events.8100 = {
	type = character_event
	title = bci_menu_relation_t
	desc = bci_events.0001.desc
	theme = default
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:currency_scope
		animation = personality_greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = set_relation_debug_friend
		trigger = {
			NOT = { scope:actor_scope = scope:currency_scope }
		}
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_relation_friend = scope:actor_scope }
				remove_relation_friend = scope:actor_scope
			}
			else = {
				set_relation_friend = { reason = test_friend_desc target = scope:actor_scope }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.8100
	}
	option = {
		name = set_relation_debug_best_friend
		trigger = {
			NOT = { scope:actor_scope = scope:currency_scope }
		}
		scope:currency_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_relation_best_friend = scope:actor_scope }
				remove_relation_best_friend = scope:actor_scope
			}
			else = {
				set_relation_best_friend = { reason = friend_generic_history target = scope:actor_scope }
			}
		}
		trigger_event = bci_events.8100
	}
	option = {
		name = set_relation_debug_rival
		trigger = {
			NOT = { scope:actor_scope = scope:currency_scope }
		}
